function journeySceneEmoji(subject,areaIndex){ const sets={norwegian:['üî§','üìö','üîé','‚úíÔ∏è'],math:['üî¢','üßÆ','üìê','üß©'],english:['üéí','üí¨','üìò','üó∫Ô∏è']}; return (sets[subject]||sets.math)[areaIndex%4]; } function journeySceneDetails(subject,areaIndex){ const sets={ norwegian:[ C','üéí','üçé','üê∂'],['üí¨','üëã','üó£Ô∏è','‚úàÔ∏è'],['üìò','üîé','üåü','‚òï'],['üó∫Ô∏è','üåç','‚úíÔ∏è','üéß'] ] }; return (sets[subject]||sets.math)[areaIndex%4]; } let selectedJourneyMission=null; function journeyFoxSvg(){ return ''; } function journeySpecialSvg(kind){ if(kind==='checkpoint')return ''; if(kind==='challenge')return ''; if(kind==='review')return ''; return ''; } function journeySkillGlyph(node,isGeo=false){ if(node.type==='checkpoint'||node.type==='challenge'||node.type==='review')return ''+journeySpecialSvg(node.type)+''; if(isGeo||node.subject==='geography'){ const map={flag:'FLAG',capital:'CAP',country:'LAND',continent:'360',map:'MAP'}; return ''+(map[node.geoType]||'GEO')+''; } const s=node.subject||activeSubject,skills=node.skills||[]; let glyph='‚Ä¢',tiny=false; if(s==='math'){ if(skills.some(x=>String(x).includes('addition')))glyph='+'; else if(skills.some(x=>String(x).includes('subtraction')))glyph='‚àí'; else if(skills.includes('multiplication'))glyph='√ó'; else if(skills.includes('division'))glyph='√∑'; else if(skills.some(x=>['fractions'].includes(x)))glyph='¬Ω'; else if(skills.some(x=>['percent','percent-change','ratio'].includes(x)))glyph='%'; else if(skills.some(x=>['area','perimeter','angles','circle','volume','scale','pythagoras'].includes(x)))glyph='‚ñ≥'; else if(skills.some(x=>['algebra','expression','functions','linear','pattern'].includes(x)))glyph='x'; else {glyph='123';tiny=true} }else if(s==='norwegian'){ if(skills.some(x=>['letter-sound','missing-letter','spelling','double-consonant'].includes(x)))glyph='Aa'; else if(skills.some(x=>['word-picture','synonyms','rhyme','compound-words'].includes(x))){glyph='ORD';tiny=true} else if(skills.some(x=>['sentence-order','punctuation','grammar','word-class'].includes(x))){glyph='abc';tiny=true} else if(skills.some(x=>String(x).startsWith('reading'))){glyph='LES';tiny=true} else } function learningFeedbackText(q,correct){ if(correct){ if(q.subject==='english'&&q.englishWord&&q.norwegianMeaning) return q.englishWord+' betyr '+q.norwegianMeaning+'.'; return q.subject==='english'?'Du fant riktig svar.':'Bra! } function hideRetryFeedback(){ if(!wrongAttempt)return; wrongAttempt=false; const box=wrap.querySelector('#feedback');if(box)box.style.display='none'; const next=wrap.querySelector('#next-question');if(next)next.style.display='none'; } function placeLetter(letterIdx,slotIdx){ if(currentAnswered||letterIdx===null||slotIdx===null||Number.isNaN(letterIdx)||Number.isNaN(slotIdx))return; hideRetryFeedback(); const from=slots.findIndex(x=>x&&x.idx===letterIdx); }); } function helperText(){ if(selectedLetterIdx!==null)return 'Velg hvilken rute '+letters[selectedLetterIdx].l+' skal st√• i.'; if(selectedSlotIdx!==null)return 'Velg bokstaven som skal st√• i rute '+(selectedSlotIdx+1)+'.'; } function setGlobeZoom(next){ const maxZoom=activeGlobeZoomMax(); globeZoom=Math.max(GLOBE_ZOOM_MIN,Math.min(maxZoom,next)); const zin=document.getElementById('globe-zoom-in'),zout=document.getElementById('globe-zoom-out'); globeMove=20; } function initGlobe(){const canvas=document.getElementById('globe-canvas');if(!canvas)return;resizeGlobe();drawGlobe();if(canvas.dataset.ready)return;canvas.dataset.ready='1'; let raf=0,pendingDraw=false,lastMoveAt=0; window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(document.getElementById('world-screen').classList.contains('active')){resizeGlobe();drawGlobe()}}); } function resizeGlobe(){const canvas=document.getElementById('globe-canvas');if(!canvas)return;const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();/* A short landscape screen can legitimately show a 230px round globe. Never force a 280px bitmap against its smaller CSS bounds: that breaks coordinate-accurate touch and WebKit projection. */const realW=rect.width>=100?rect.width:(window.innerWidth||360),realH=rect.height>=100?rect.height:(window.innerHeight||realW),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(realW)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(realH)),dpr=Math.min(2,window.devicePixelRatio||1);canvas.width=Math.round(w*dpr);canvas.height=Math.round(h*dpr);canvas._cssW=w;canvas._cssH=h;canvas._cssSize=Math.min(w,h);canvas._dpr=dpr} function globeProject(lon,lat,w,h){const canvas=document.getElementById('globe-canvas');w=w||canvas?._cssW||360;h=h||canvas?._cssH||w;const rad=Math.PI/180,l=(lon-globeLon)*rad,p=lat*rad,p0=globeLat*rad,cp=Math.cos(p),sp=Math.sin(p),c0=Math.cos(p0),s0=Math.sin(p0),cosc=s0*sp+c0*cp*Math.cos(l);if(cosc<=0)return null;const r=Math.min(w,h)*.455*globeZoom;return [w/2+r*cp*Math.sin(l),h/2-r*(c0*sp-s0*cp*Math.cos(l)),cosc]} function drawProjectedLine(ctx,coords,w,h){let started=false,last=null;const moving=window.__lariaGlobeInteracting===true,maxPts=moving?(globeZoom<1.6?72:globeZoom<3?110:170):(globeZoom<1.6?180:globeZoom<3?320:700),step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(coords.length/maxPts)),jump=Math.min(w,h)*.7;for(let i=0;ijump){ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1]);started=true}else ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]);last=p}if(step>1&&coords.length){const pt=coords[coords.length-1],p=globeProject(pt[0],pt[1],w,h);if(p&&last&&Math.hypot(p[0]-last[0],p[1]-last[1])<=jump)ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1])}} function geometrySpan(c){if(!c?.geometry)return 0;if(Number.isFinite(c._geometrySpan))return c._geometrySpan;let minLat=90,maxLat=-90,minLon=Infinity,maxLon=-Infinity;const ref=Number.isFinite(c.lon)?c.lon:0;for(const poly of geometryPolygons(c.geometry))for(const ring of poly)for(const pt of ring){const lon=normalizeLon(pt[0],ref);minLon=Math.min(minLon,lon);maxLon=Math.max(maxLon,lon);minLat=Math.min(minLat,pt[1]);maxLat=Math.max(maxLat,pt[1])}c._geometrySpan=Math.max(maxLon-minLon,maxLat-minLat);return c._geometrySpan} function isTinyCountry(c){const span=geometrySpan(c);return !c.geometry||span<2.2} function countryFocusZoom(c){const span=geometrySpan(c);if(!span||span<1)return 4.4;if(span<3)return 4;if(span<8)return 3.2;if(span<18)return 2.6;if(span<35)return 2.05;if(span<65)return 1.65;return 1.38} function drawGlobe(){const canvas=document.getElementById('globe-canvas');if(!canvas||!canvas._cssW||!canvas._cssH)return;const w=canvas._cssW,h=canvas._cssH,s=Math.min(w,h),dpr=canvas._dpr,ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(w/2,h/2,s*.46,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.clip(); lon=-180;lon<=180;lon+=4)pts.push([lon,lat]);drawProjectedLine(ctx,pts,w,h);ctx.stroke()} for(let lon=-180;lon<180;lon+=30){ctx.beginPath();const pts=[];for(let poly){drawProjectedLine(ctx,ring,w,h)}}ctx.fillStyle=country.id===globeSelected?'#FFD869':globeCountryColor(country);ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.82)';ctx.lineWidth=globeZoom>2?1.05:.75;ctx.fill();ctx.stroke()} ctx.restore();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(w/2,h/2,s*.46,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.95)';ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.stroke(); if(pulsing)requestAnimationFrame(drawGlobe); } function globeInverse(x,y){const canvas=document.getElementById('globe-canvas'),w=canvas._cssW||canvas._cssSize,h=canvas._cssH||canvas._cssSize,s=Math.min(w,h),r=s*.455*globeZoom,X=(x-w/2)/r,Y=-(y-h/2)/r,rho=Math.hypot(X,Y);if(rho>1)return function drawFlatCountry(ctx,c,project,fill,stroke='#fff'){const geom=c?._detailGeometry||c?.geometry;if(!geom)return;ctx.beginPath();for(const poly of geometryPolygons(geom)){for(const ring of poly){let first=true,last=null,step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(ring.length/450));for(let i=0;i1&&ring.length){const p=project(ring[ring.length-1][0],ring[ring.length-1][1]);if(p&&last)ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1])}ctx.closePath()}}ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.strokeStyle=stroke;ctx.lineWidth=1.2;ctx.fill();ctx.stroke()} function quizBounds(q){if(q.mapIds?.length===NORDIC_IDS.length&&NORDIC_IDS.every(id=>q.mapIds.includes(id)))return MAP_BOUNDS.Norden;return MAP_BOUNDS[q.mapRegion]||MAP_BOUNDS[countries[q.k]?.continent]||MAP_BOUNDS.Europa} function renderQuizMap(q,answered=currentAnswered){const canvas=document.getElementById('quiz-map-canvas');if(!canvas)return; if(!journeyNodeIsCompleteForWorldReaction(node,isGeo))return; window.__journeyWorldReaction={subject,grade,nodeId:node.id,areaId:node.areaId,at:Date.now()}; } document.getElementById('complete-home').onclick=()=>{ const scope=lastCompletedScope||{}; armJourneyWorldReaction(scope); if(scope.type==='subject'&&scope.subject==='english'){openSubject('english');return} if(scope.type==='journey'&&scope.subject&&scope.subject!=='geography'){openSubject(scope.subject);return} if(scope.type==='geo-journey'||scope.subject==='geography'){renderGeographyContinue();showScreen('geography');return} if(currentGrade()<=2){setTab('progress');return} returnToLearningHome(); --- Quality-assured external data layer --- const NE50_URL='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nvkelso/natural-earth-vector/master/geojson/ne_50m_admin_0_countries.geojson'; const WB2024_URL='https://api.worldbank.org/v2/country/all/indicator/SP.POP.TOTL?date=2024&format=json&per_page=400'; const qualityStatus={map:false,population:false}; function updateDataStatus(){const el=document.getElementById('data-status');if(!el)return;if(qualityStatus.map&&qualityStatus.population)el.textContent='Kart: Natural Earth 1:50m ¬∑ befolkning: Verdensbanken 2024';else if(qualityStatus.map)el.textContent='Kart: Natural Earth 1:50m ¬∑ befolkning bruker reserveverdier';else if(qualityStatus.population)el.textContent='Befolkning: Verdensbanken 2024 ¬∑ kart bruker innebygd reserve';else el.textContent='Norske navn og hovedsteder ¬∑ innebygde reservefakta er aktive offline'} function findCountryForFeature(p={}){const a2=[p.ISO_A2_EH,p.ISO_A2,p.WB_A2].find(v=>typeof v==='string'&&v.length===2&&v!=='-99');if(a2){const hit=WORLD_COUNTRIES.find(c=>c.a2===a2);if(hit)return hit}const a3=[p.ISO_A3_EH,p.ADM0_A3,p.ISO_A3,p.SOV_A3].find(v=>typeof v==='string'&&v.length===3&&v!=='-99');return a3?WORLD_COUNTRIES.find(c=>c.a3===a3):null} async function loadDetailedGeometry(){try{const r=await fetch(NE50_URL,{cache:'force-cache'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('map '+r.status);const geo=await r.json();let updated=0;for(const f of geo.features||[]){const c=findCountryForFeature(f.properties);if(!c||!f.geometry)continue;c._detailGeometry=f.geometry;const px=Number(f.properties?.LABEL_X),py=Number(f.properties?.LABEL_Y);if(Number.isFinite(px)&&Number.isFinite(py)){c.lon=px;c.lat=py}updated++}qualityStatus.map=updated>150;updateDataStatus()}catch(e){updateDataStatus()}} async function loadPopulation2024(){try{const r=await fetch(WB2024_URL,{cache:'force-cache'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error('population '+r.status);const data=await r.json(),rows=Array.isArray(data)&&Array.isArray(data[1])?data[1]:[];let updated=0;for(const row of rows){if(!row||row.value==null)continue;const code=String(row.countryiso3code||'').toUpperCase(),c=WORLD_COUNTRIES.find(x=>x.a3===code);if(!c)continue;c.population=Number(row.value);c.populationYear=2024;updated++}qualityStatus.population=updated>150;updateDataStatus()}catch(e){updateDataStatus()}} let qualityDataPromise=null; function loadQualityData(){ if(qualityDataPromise)return qualityDataPromise; updateDataStatus(); qualityDataPromise=Promise.allSettled([loadDetailedGeometry(),loadPopulation2024()]).then(()=>{updateDataStatus();return true}); return qualityDataPromise; } let qualityDataScheduled=false; function scheduleQualityData(){ if(qualityDataPromise||qualityDataScheduled)return; qualityDataScheduled=true; setTimeout(()=>{ const run=()=>loadQualityData().catch(()=>{}); if('requestIdleCallback' in window)requestIdleCallback(run,{timeout:6000}); else setTimeout(run,1200); },2500); } // PWA registration: network-first navigation in sw.js prevents stale HTML/JS mixes after deploy. if('serviceWorker' in navigator){ window.addEventListener('load',()=>navigator.serviceWorker.register('./sw.js',{updateViaCache:'none'}).catch(()=>{})); } // App resume policy: // - A real launch always starts on Home. // - Short background pauses keep the current screen. // - After 10 minutes away, return to Home while preserving any unfinished session. const APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS=10*60*1000; let appHiddenAt=null; function returnHomeAfterLongPause(){ if(activeScreenName()==='session'&&state.activeSession)persistActiveSession(); setTab('home'); renderHomeContinue(); } document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{ if(document.visibilityState==='hidden'){ appHiddenAt=Date.now(); if(activeScreenName()==='session'&&state.activeSession)persistActiveSession(); return; } if(document.visibilityState==='visible'&&appHiddenAt){ const awayFor=Date.now()-appHiddenAt; appHiddenAt=null; if(awayFor>=APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS)returnHomeAfterLongPause(); } }); window.addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{ appHiddenAt=Date.now(); if(activeScreenName()==='session'&&state.activeSession)persistActiveSession(); }); window.addEventListener('pageshow',event=>{ if(!event.persisted)return; const awayFor=appHiddenAt?Date.now()-appHiddenAt:APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS; appHiddenAt=null; if(awayFor>=APP_RESUME_HOME_AFTER_MS)returnHomeAfterLongPause(); }); // Initial render / recovery
